I don't know, it's just like the color is turned down on everything.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself enjoying things you used to like, such as hobbies or spending time with friends and family, as much as you used to?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I just don't really care to do any of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you often worry about things that are not related to your job?\n\nPatient: No. Not really. I mean... no more than anyone else, I guess. I don't really sit around worrying about things.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed a decrease in pleasure from activities that you normally find enjoyable?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I mean... like I just said a minute ago. With the grilling and all that. I just don't really get anything out of it right now.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any change in your weight recently?\n\nPatient: I guess so. My belt is a little looser lately... not that I'm trying to lose weight or anything. I just haven't had much of an appetite. I usually just grab a few bites here and there, or... I don't know, just skip supper.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts of not wanting to be alive or of harming yourself?\n\nPatient: I mean... no. I don't really track the days. They're just my own thoughts.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any days in the past month where you felt completely back to normal?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I don't think so.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
